, the proof follows exact the same guidelines as in the Proof of Theorem
3
, case (i) but using the nuclear norm
\mathcal{R}:=\|\cdot\|_{N}
. We highlight next the minor changes. First,
\mathscr{G}(\mathfrak{S}^{1/2}(\mathbb{B}_{\|\cdot\|_{N}}))\leq\rho_{N}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma})(\sqrt{d_{1}}+\sqrt{d_{2}})
by Lemma H.1 in
[
68
]
— up to an absolute constant. As a consequence, in the constants
\{\mathsf{a}_{i}\}
,
\{\mathsf{b}_{i}\}
,
\{\mathsf{c}_{i}\}
,
\{\mathsf{d}_{i}\}
and
\{\mathsf{f}_{i}\}
, we have to replace the factor
\rho_{1}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma})\sqrt{\frac{\log p}{n}}
by
\rho_{N}(\boldsymbol{\Sigma})\sqrt{\frac{d_{1}+d_{2}}{n}}
. Second, we have
\Psi_{\|\cdot\|_{N}}(\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{B}}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}))\leq\sqrt{r}
— so that we replace
\mu(\boldsymbol{b})=\mu\left(\mathcal{C}_{\boldsymbol{b},\|\cdot\|_{1}}(6)\right)
by
\mu(\mathbf{B})=\mu\left(\mathcal{C}_{\mathbf{B},\|\cdot\|_{N}}(6)\right)
.

33
Proof of Theorem
21

Throughout this section
\mathcal{R}
is a decomposable norm on

\mathds{R}^{p}
and we grant Assumption
1
and model (
2
). We set

\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}:=\boldsymbol{y}-\mathfrak{X}(\hat{\mathbf{B}})-\sqrt{n}\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}
,

\boldsymbol{\Delta}:=\mathfrak{X}(\hat{\mathbf{B}})-\boldsymbol{f}
and
\hat{\sigma}:=\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}\|_{2}
. Given
\mathbf{B}
, we define the quantities

\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\mathbf{B}}:=\boldsymbol{y}-\mathfrak{X}(\mathbf{B})-\sqrt{n}\boldsymbol{\theta}^{*}

and

\mathcal{E}_{\mathbf{B}}:=\mathfrak{X}(\mathbf{B})-\boldsymbol{f}.

Note that
\boldsymbol{\xi}=\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\mathbf{B}}+\mathcal{E}_{\mathbf{B}}
.

Lemma 31
.

For all
\mathbf{B}\in\mathds{R}^{p}
, it holds that

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\leq\langle\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}},\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})\rangle+(\hat{\sigma}\lambda)\big(\mathcal{R}(\mathbf{B})-\mathcal{R}(\hat{\mathbf{B}})\big)+(\hat{\sigma}\tau)\big(\|\boldsymbol{\theta}^{*}\|_{\sharp}-\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}\|_{\sharp}\big)

(377)

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{E}_{\mathbf{B}}^{(n)}\|_{2}\left(\lambda\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})+\tau\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{\sharp}\right).

(378)

and also

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{(n)}+\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}},\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\langle\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)},\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})\rangle

(379)

\displaystyle+(\hat{\sigma}\lambda)\big(\mathcal{R}(\mathbf{B})-\mathcal{R}(\hat{\mathbf{B}})\big)+(\hat{\sigma}\tau)\big(\|\boldsymbol{\theta}^{*}\|_{\sharp}-\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}\|_{\sharp}\big)

(380)

\displaystyle+\left(\|\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})\|_{2}+\|\mathcal{E}_{\mathbf{B}}^{(n)}\|_{2}\right)\left(\lambda\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})+\tau\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{\sharp}\right).

(381)

Proof.

We first prove (
378
). It is enough to assume

\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\mathbf{B}}\neq\mathbf{0}
. Comparing the minimality of

[\hat{\mathbf{B}},\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}]
with
[\mathbf{B},\boldsymbol{\theta}^{*}]
and by convexity of

\boldsymbol{z}\mapsto\|\boldsymbol{z}\|_{2}
,

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}}\|_{2}-\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}\|_{2}+\lambda\big(\mathcal{R}(\mathbf{B})-\mathcal{R}(\hat{\mathbf{B}})\big)+\tau\big(\|\boldsymbol{\theta}^{*}\|_{\sharp}-\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}\|_{\sharp}\big)

(382)

\displaystyle\leq-\left\langle\frac{\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}}}{\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}}\|_{2}},\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}-\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}}\right\rangle+\lambda\big(\mathcal{R}(\mathbf{B})-\mathcal{R}(\hat{\mathbf{B}})\big)+\tau\big(\|\boldsymbol{\theta}^{*}\|_{\sharp}-\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}\|_{\sharp}\big).

(383)

Using
\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}}=\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}+\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})
we get

\displaystyle 0\leq\langle\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}},\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})\rangle+\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}}\|_{2}\lambda\big(\mathcal{R}(\mathbf{B})-\mathcal{R}(\hat{\mathbf{B}})\big)+\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}}\|_{2}\tau\big(\|\boldsymbol{\theta}^{*}\|_{\sharp}-\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}\|_{\sharp}\big).

(384)

We now write

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}}\|_{2}\lambda\big(\mathcal{R}(\mathbf{B})-\mathcal{R}(\hat{\mathbf{B}})\big)+\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}}\|_{2}\tau\big(\|\boldsymbol{\theta}^{*}\|_{\sharp}-\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}\|_{\sharp}\big)

(385)

\displaystyle=\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}\|_{2}\lambda\big(\mathcal{R}(\mathbf{B})-\mathcal{R}(\hat{\mathbf{B}})\big)+\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}\|_{2}\tau\big(\|\boldsymbol{\theta}^{*}\|_{\sharp}-\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}\|_{\sharp}\big)

(386)

\displaystyle+(\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}}\|_{2}-\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}\|_{2})\lambda\big(\mathcal{R}(\mathbf{B})-\mathcal{R}(\hat{\mathbf{B}})\big)+(\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}}\|_{2}-\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}\|_{2})\tau\big(\|\boldsymbol{\theta}^{*}\|_{\sharp}-\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}\|_{\sharp}\big).

(387)

By convexity of
\mathbf{W}\mapsto\mathcal{R}(\mathbf{W})
and

\boldsymbol{\theta}\mapsto\|\boldsymbol{\theta}\|_{\sharp}
, there exist
\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{B}}\in\mathcal{R}(\mathbf{B})
and
\boldsymbol{u}_{\boldsymbol{\theta}^{*}}\in\partial\|\boldsymbol{\theta}^{*}\|_{\sharp}
such that

\displaystyle(\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}}\|_{2}-\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}\|_{2})\lambda\big(\mathcal{R}(\mathbf{B})-\mathcal{R}(\hat{\mathbf{B}})\big)+(\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}}\|_{2}-\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}\|_{2})\tau\big(\|\boldsymbol{\theta}^{*}\|_{\sharp}-\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}\|_{\sharp}\big)

(388)

\displaystyle\leq(\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}}\|_{2}-\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}\|_{2})\lambda\left(-\langle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\langle\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{B}},\hat{\mathbf{B}}-\mathbf{B}\rangle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\rangle\right)+(\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}}\|_{2}-\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}\|_{2})\tau\left(-\langle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\langle\boldsymbol{u}_{\boldsymbol{\theta}^{*}},\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}-\boldsymbol{\theta}^{*}\rangle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\rangle\right)

(389)

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathcal{E}_{\mathbf{B}}^{(n)}\|_{2}\lambda\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})+\|\mathcal{E}_{\mathbf{B}}^{(n)}\|_{2}\tau\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{\sharp},

(390)

where we used that

\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\mathbf{B}}=\boldsymbol{\xi}-\mathcal{E}_{\mathbf{B}},

|\langle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\langle\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}\rangle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\rangle|\leq\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})
and

|\langle\boldsymbol{u}_{\boldsymbol{\theta}^{*}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\rangle|\leq\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{\sharp}
. Equation (
378
) follow from the three previous displays.

We now show (
381
). The first order condition of (
3
) at
[\hat{\mathbf{B}},\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}]
is equivalent to the statement: there exist

\mathbf{V}\in\partial\mathcal{R}(\hat{\mathbf{B}})
and
\boldsymbol{u}\in\partial\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}\|_{\sharp}
such that for all
[\mathbf{B},\boldsymbol{\theta}]
,

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in[n]}\left[y_{i}^{(n)}-\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{B}})-\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}_{i}\right]\langle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\langle\mathbf{X}^{(n)}_{i},\hat{\mathbf{B}}-\mathbf{B}\rangle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\lambda\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}\|_{2}\langle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\langle\mathbf{V},\hat{\mathbf{B}}-\mathbf{B}\rangle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\rangle,

(391)

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{y}^{(n)}-\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\hat{\mathbf{B}})-\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}},\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}-\boldsymbol{\theta}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\tau\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}\|_{2}\langle\boldsymbol{u},\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}-\boldsymbol{\theta}\rangle.

(392)

Setting
\boldsymbol{\theta}=\boldsymbol{\theta}^{*}
and using that

\boldsymbol{y}^{(n)}=\boldsymbol{f}^{(n)}+\boldsymbol{\theta}^{*}+\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}

we obtain that for all
\mathbf{B}
,

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in[n]}\left[\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i}^{(n)}+\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{i}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\right]\langle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\langle\mathbf{X}^{(n)}_{i},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}\rangle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in[n]}\xi_{i}^{(n)}\langle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\langle\mathbf{X}_{i}^{(n)},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}\rangle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\rangle-\lambda\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}\|_{2}\langle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\langle\mathbf{V},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}\rangle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\rangle,

(393)

\displaystyle\left\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{(n)}+\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\langle\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\rangle-\tau\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}\|_{2}\langle\boldsymbol{u},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\rangle.

(394)

Summing the above inequalities,

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{(n)}+\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}},\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\langle\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)},\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})\rangle

(395)

\displaystyle-\lambda\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}\|_{2}\langle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\langle\mathbf{V},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}\rangle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\rangle-\tau\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}\|_{2}\langle\boldsymbol{u},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\rangle.

(396)

We now write

\displaystyle-\lambda\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}\|_{2}\langle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\langle\mathbf{V},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}\rangle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\rangle-\tau\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}\|_{2}\langle\boldsymbol{u},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\rangle

(397)

\displaystyle=-\lambda\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}\|_{2}\langle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\langle\mathbf{V},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}\rangle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\rangle-\tau\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}\|_{2}\langle\boldsymbol{u},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\rangle

(398)

\displaystyle+\lambda(\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}\|_{2}-\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}\|_{2})\langle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\langle\mathbf{V},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}\rangle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\rangle+\tau(\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}\|_{2}-\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}\|_{2})\langle\boldsymbol{u},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\rangle

(399)

Using that
-\langle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\mathbf{V}\rangle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\rangle\leq\mathcal{R}(\mathbf{B})-\mathcal{R}(\hat{\mathbf{B}})
and
-\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}},\boldsymbol{u}\rangle\leq\|\boldsymbol{\theta}^{*}\|_{\sharp}-\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}\|_{\sharp}
, we get that

\displaystyle-\lambda\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}\|_{2}\langle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\langle\mathbf{V},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}\rangle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\rangle-\tau\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}\|_{2}\langle\boldsymbol{u},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\rangle\leq(\hat{\sigma}\lambda)\big(\mathcal{R}(\mathbf{B})-\mathcal{R}(\hat{\mathbf{B}})\big)+(\hat{\sigma}\tau)\big(\|\boldsymbol{\theta}^{*}\|_{\sharp}-\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}\|_{\sharp}\big).

(400)

Finally, using the facts that
\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}=\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}-\mathcal{E}_{\mathbf{B}}^{(n)}-\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})
,

|\langle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\langle\mathbf{V},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}\rangle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\rangle|\leq\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})
and

|\langle\boldsymbol{u},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\rangle|\leq\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{\sharp}

we get

\displaystyle\lambda(\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}\|_{2}-\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}\|_{2})\langle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\langle\mathbf{V},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}\rangle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\rangle+\tau(\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}\|_{2}-\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}\|_{2})\langle\boldsymbol{u},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\rangle

(401)

\displaystyle\leq\left(\|\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})\|_{2}+\|\mathcal{E}_{\mathbf{B}}^{(n)}\|_{2}\right)\left(\lambda\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})+\tau\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{\sharp}\right).

(402)

Equation (
381
) follows from the four previous displays.
∎

Next, we upper (and lower) bound (
381
) using
\MP
(and
\ATP
).

Lemma 32
.

Suppose conditions (i)-(ii) of Theorem
21
hold. Let
\mathbf{B}\in\mathds{R}^{p}
and
define the quantities

\displaystyle\blacktriangle_{1}

\displaystyle:=\left(\mathsf{f}_{2}+\frac{\mathsf{f}_{1}\mathsf{d}_{2}}{\mathsf{d}_{1}}+\|\mathcal{E}_{\mathbf{B}}^{(n)}\|_{2}\lambda\right)\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})+\left(\mathsf{f}_{4}+\frac{\mathsf{f}_{1}\mathsf{d}_{4}}{\mathsf{d}_{1}}+\|\mathcal{E}_{\mathbf{B}}^{(n)}\|_{2}\tau\right)\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{\sharp},

(403)

\displaystyle\blacktriangledown_{1}

\displaystyle:=(\hat{\sigma}\lambda)\big(\mathcal{R}(\mathbf{B})-\mathcal{R}(\hat{\mathbf{B}})\big)+(\hat{\sigma}\tau)\big(\|\boldsymbol{\theta}^{*}\|_{\sharp}-\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}\|_{\sharp}\big).

(404)

Then

\displaystyle 0\leq\left(\frac{\mathsf{f}_{1}}{\mathsf{d}_{1}}+\|\mathcal{E}_{\mathbf{B}}^{(n)}\|_{2}\right)\|\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})\|_{2}+\blacktriangle_{1}+\blacktriangledown_{1},

(405)

and also

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{(n)}+\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\mathbf{B})-\boldsymbol{f}^{(n)}\|_{2}^{2}

(406)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\mathsf{f}_{1}}{\mathsf{d}_{1}}\|\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})\|_{2}+2(\blacktriangle_{1}+\blacktriangledown_{1})

(407)

\displaystyle+2\|\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})\|_{2}\left(\lambda\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})+\tau\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{\sharp}\right).

(408)

Proof.

By the parallelogram law,

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{(n)}+\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}},\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})\rangle=

(409)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{(n)}+\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\|\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})\|_{2}^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\|\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\mathbf{B})-\boldsymbol{f}^{(n)}\|_{2}^{2}.

(410)

\ATP
implies in particular that

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})\|_{2}

\displaystyle\geq\mathsf{d}_{1}\|[\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}]\|_{\Pi}-\mathsf{d}_{2}\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})-\mathsf{d}_{4}\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{\sharp}.

(411)

\MP
implies that

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)},\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\mathsf{f}_{1}\|[\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}]\|_{\Pi}+\mathsf{f}_{2}\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})+\mathsf{f}_{4}\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{\sharp}.

(412)

The proof of (
408
) follows from the three previous displays and inequality (
381
) of Lemma
31
.

The proof of (
405
) follows from the two previous displays, inequality (
378
) and the fact that

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}},\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)},\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})\rangle-\langle\mathcal{E}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}},\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})\rangle

(413)

\displaystyle\leq\langle\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)},\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})\rangle+\|\mathcal{E}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}}\|_{2}\|\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})\|_{2}.

(414)

∎

The previous lemmas entail the next proposition. For convenience, given
[\mathbf{V},\mathbf{B},\boldsymbol{u}]
, we define

\displaystyle\triangle_{\lambda,\tau}(\mathbf{V},\boldsymbol{u}|\mathbf{B})

\displaystyle:=(\nicefrac{{3\lambda}}{{2}})(\mathcal{R}\circ\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{B}})(\mathbf{V})-(\nicefrac{{\lambda}}{{2}})(\mathcal{R}\circ\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{B}}^{\perp})(\mathbf{V})

(415)

\displaystyle+(\nicefrac{{3\tau\Omega}}{{2}})\|\boldsymbol{u}\|_{2}-(\nicefrac{{\tau}}{{2}})\sum_{i=o+1}^{n}\omega_{i}\boldsymbol{u}_{i}^{\sharp}.

(416)

Proposition 8
.

Suppose the conditions (i)-(ii) of Theorem
21
hold and, additionally, for some
\mathsf{c}_{n}\in[0,1/4)
,

(iii’)

(1-4\mathsf{c}_{n})\hat{\sigma}\lambda\geq 4[\mathsf{f}_{2}+(\nicefrac{{\mathsf{f}_{1}\mathsf{d}_{2}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}})]

and

(1-4\mathsf{c}_{n})\hat{\sigma}\tau\geq 4[\mathsf{f}_{4}+(\nicefrac{{\mathsf{f}_{1}\mathsf{d}_{4}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}})].

(iv’)

56\left(\nicefrac{{\mathsf{f}_{1}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}}+\hat{\sigma}\mathsf{c}_{n}\right)\leq 3\hat{\sigma}.

For any
D\geq 0
and
\mathbf{B}
satisfying the constraints
(
91
)-(
92
),

\displaystyle(\nicefrac{{\hat{\sigma}}}{{2}})(\lambda\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})+\tau\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{\sharp})+\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{(n)}+\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq D^{2}+\spadesuit_{1}\left(\mathsf{f}_{1},R\right),

(417)

where
r:=r_{\hat{\sigma}\lambda,\hat{\sigma}\tau\Omega,6}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}|\mathbf{B})
and
R:=(\hat{\sigma}\mathsf{c}_{n})\vee(3r)
. Moreover,

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})\|_{2}\leq 2D+\clubsuit_{1}\left(\mathsf{f}_{1},R\right).

(418)

Proof.

Let

\blacksquare_{1}:=(\nicefrac{{\hat{\sigma}\lambda}}{{4}})\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})+(\nicefrac{{\hat{\sigma}\tau}}{{4}})\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{\sharp}
.
By Lemma
32
and
\|\mathcal{E}_{\mathbf{B}}^{(n)}\|_{2}\leq\hat{\sigma}\mathsf{c}_{n}
, we have

\displaystyle 0\leq\left(\frac{\mathsf{f}_{1}}{\mathsf{d}_{1}}+\hat{\sigma}\mathsf{c}_{n}\right)\|\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})\|_{2}+\blacktriangle_{1}+\blacktriangledown_{1},

(419)

and also

\displaystyle 2\blacksquare_{1}+\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{(n)}+\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\mathbf{B})-\boldsymbol{f}^{(n)}\|_{2}^{2}

(420)

\displaystyle+(\nicefrac{{2\mathsf{f}_{1}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}})\|\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})\|_{2}+2(\blacktriangle_{1}+\blacksquare_{1}+\blacktriangledown_{1})

(421)

\displaystyle+2\|\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})\|_{2}\left(\lambda\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})+\tau\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{\sharp}\right).

(422)

By Lemmas
26
and
27
(with

) and conditions (iii’) and

,

\displaystyle\blacktriangle_{1}+\blacksquare_{1}+\blacktriangledown_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\left[\mathsf{f}_{2}+\frac{\mathsf{f}_{1}\mathsf{d}_{2}}{\mathsf{d}_{1}}+\hat{\sigma}\lambda\mathsf{c}_{n}+(\nicefrac{{\hat{\sigma}\lambda}}{{4}})\right]\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})+(\hat{\sigma}\lambda)\big(\mathcal{R}(\mathbf{B})-\mathcal{R}(\hat{\mathbf{B}})\big)

(423)

\displaystyle+\left[\mathsf{f}_{4}+\frac{\mathsf{f}_{1}\mathsf{d}_{4}}{\mathsf{d}_{1}}+\hat{\sigma}\tau\mathsf{c}_{n}+(\nicefrac{{\hat{\sigma}\tau}}{{4}})\right]\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{\sharp}+(\hat{\sigma}\tau)\big(\|\boldsymbol{\theta}^{*}\|_{\sharp}-\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}\|_{\sharp}\big)

(424)

\displaystyle\leq(\nicefrac{{\hat{\sigma}\lambda}}{{2}})\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})+(\hat{\sigma}\lambda)\big(\mathcal{R}(\mathbf{B})-\mathcal{R}(\hat{\mathbf{B}})\big)

(425)

\displaystyle+(\nicefrac{{\hat{\sigma}\tau}}{{2}})\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{\sharp}+(\hat{\sigma}\tau)\big(\|\boldsymbol{\theta}^{*}\|_{\sharp}-\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}\|_{\sharp}\big)

(426)

\displaystyle\leq\triangle_{\hat{\sigma}\lambda,\hat{\sigma}\tau}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}|\mathbf{B}).

(427)

Next, we will define some local variables for convenience of notation. Let

G:=\|\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})\|_{2}
,

D:=\|\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\mathbf{B})-\boldsymbol{f}^{(n)}\|_{2},

x:=\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{(n)}+\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{2},

and

r:=r_{\hat{\sigma}\lambda,\hat{\sigma}\tau\Omega,6}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}|\mathbf{B})
. Define also

\triangle:=\triangle_{\hat{\sigma}\lambda,\hat{\sigma}\tau}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}|\mathbf{B})
and

\displaystyle H

\displaystyle:=(\nicefrac{{3\hat{\sigma}\lambda}}{{2}})(\mathcal{R}\circ\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{B}})(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})+(\nicefrac{{3\hat{\sigma}\tau\Omega}}{{2}})\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{2},

(428)

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle:=(\nicefrac{{\hat{\sigma}\lambda}}{{2}})(\mathcal{R}\circ\mathcal{P}_{\mathbf{B}}^{\perp})(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})+(\nicefrac{{\hat{\sigma}\tau}}{{2}})\sum_{i=o+1}^{n}\omega_{i}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})_{i}^{\sharp}.

(429)

In particular,

\triangle=H-I.

The previous three bounds entail the two inequalities:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\leq\left(\nicefrac{{\mathsf{f}_{1}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}}+\hat{\sigma}\mathsf{c}_{n}\right)G+\triangle,

(430)

\displaystyle 2\blacksquare_{1}+x^{2}+G^{2}

\displaystyle\leq D^{2}+\left(\nicefrac{{2\mathsf{f}_{1}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}}\right)G+2\left(\lambda\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})+\tau\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{\sharp}\right)G+2\triangle.

(431)

We split our argument in two cases.

Case 1:

\left(\nicefrac{{\mathsf{f}_{1}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}}+\hat{\sigma}\mathsf{c}_{n}\right)G\geq H
. Hence,
\triangle\leq H\leq\left(\nicefrac{{\mathsf{f}_{1}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}}+\hat{\sigma}\mathsf{c}_{n}\right)G
. From (
430
),

I\leq\left(\nicefrac{{\mathsf{f}_{1}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}}+\hat{\sigma}\mathsf{c}_{n}\right)G+H\leq 2\left(\nicefrac{{\mathsf{f}_{1}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}}+\hat{\sigma}\mathsf{c}_{n}\right)G.

This fact and decomposability imply

\displaystyle\hat{\sigma}\left(\lambda\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})+\tau\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{\sharp}\right)\leq 2I+\frac{2H}{3}\leq\frac{14}{3}\left(\nicefrac{{\mathsf{f}_{1}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}}+\hat{\sigma}\mathsf{c}_{n}\right)G.

(432)

From (
431
), we get

\displaystyle 2\blacksquare_{1}+x^{2}+G^{2}

\displaystyle\leq D^{2}+\left(\nicefrac{{2\mathsf{f}_{1}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}}\right)G+\frac{28}{3\hat{\sigma}}\left(\nicefrac{{\mathsf{f}_{1}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}}+\hat{\sigma}\mathsf{c}_{n}\right)G^{2}+2\triangle

(433)

\displaystyle\leq D^{2}+2\left(\nicefrac{{2\mathsf{f}_{1}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}}+\hat{\sigma}\mathsf{c}_{n}\right)G+\frac{28}{3\hat{\sigma}}\left(\nicefrac{{\mathsf{f}_{1}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}}+\hat{\sigma}\mathsf{c}_{n}\right)G^{2},

(434)

which, together with condition (iv’), implies

\displaystyle 2\blacksquare_{1}+x^{2}+\frac{G^{2}}{2}

\displaystyle\leq D^{2}+2\left(\nicefrac{{2\mathsf{f}_{1}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}}+\hat{\sigma}\mathsf{c}_{n}\right)G.

(435)

From
2\left(\nicefrac{{2\mathsf{f}_{1}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}}+\hat{\sigma}\mathsf{c}_{n}\right)G\leq 2\left(\nicefrac{{2\mathsf{f}_{1}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}}+\hat{\sigma}\mathsf{c}_{n}\right)^{2}+\frac{G^{2}}{2}
, we get

\displaystyle 2\blacksquare_{1}+x^{2}

\displaystyle\leq D^{2}+2\left(\nicefrac{{2\mathsf{f}_{1}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}}+\hat{\sigma}\mathsf{c}_{n}\right)^{2}.

(436)

If we use instead
2\left(\nicefrac{{2\mathsf{f}_{1}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}}+\hat{\sigma}\mathsf{c}_{n}\right)G\leq 4\left(\nicefrac{{2\mathsf{f}_{1}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}}+\hat{\sigma}\mathsf{c}_{n}\right)^{2}+\frac{G^{2}}{4}
, we get

\displaystyle\frac{G^{2}}{4}

\displaystyle\leq D^{2}+4\left(\nicefrac{{2\mathsf{f}_{1}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}}+\hat{\sigma}\mathsf{c}_{n}\right)^{2}.

(437)

Case 2:

\left(\nicefrac{{\mathsf{f}_{1}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}}+\hat{\sigma}\mathsf{c}_{n}\right)G\leq H
. From (
430
),
0\leq H+\triangle=2H-I
so that
[\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\mathbb{0},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}]\in\mathcal{C}_{\mathbf{B},\mathbb{0}}(6,\gamma,0,\Omega)
, where we defined
\gamma:=\lambda/\tau
. By Lemma
18
,

\displaystyle\triangle

\displaystyle\leq(\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}})r\|[\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}]\|_{\Pi},

(438)

\displaystyle(\hat{\sigma}\lambda)\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})+(\hat{\sigma}\tau)\big\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\big\|_{\sharp}

\displaystyle\leq 14r\|[\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}]\|_{\Pi}.

(439)

The second inequality above and
\ATP
imply that

\displaystyle\mathsf{d}_{1}\|[\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}]\|_{\Pi}

\displaystyle\leq G+[(\nicefrac{{\mathsf{d}_{2}}}{{\lambda}})\vee(\nicefrac{{\mathsf{d}_{4}}}{{\tau}})](\lambda\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})+\tau\big\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\big\|_{\sharp})

(440)

\displaystyle\leq G+[(\nicefrac{{\mathsf{d}_{2}}}{{\lambda}})\vee(\nicefrac{{\mathsf{d}_{4}}}{{\tau}})]14(\nicefrac{{r}}{{\hat{\sigma}}})\|[\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}]\|_{\Pi}.

(441)

By condition (
92
),
14[(\nicefrac{{\mathsf{d}_{2}}}{{\lambda}})\vee(\nicefrac{{\mathsf{d}_{4}}}{{\tau}})](\nicefrac{{r}}{{\hat{\sigma}}})\leq\mathsf{d}_{1}/2
, implying
(\mathsf{d}_{1}/2)\|[\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}]\|_{\Pi}\leq G
.
We conclude that

\displaystyle\triangle

\displaystyle\leq(\nicefrac{{3r}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}})G,

(442)

\displaystyle\hat{\sigma}\lambda\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})+\hat{\sigma}\tau\big\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\big\|_{\sharp}

\displaystyle\leq(\nicefrac{{28r}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}})G.

(443)

From (
431
),

\displaystyle 2\blacksquare_{1}+x^{2}+G^{2}

\displaystyle\leq D^{2}+\left(\nicefrac{{2\mathsf{f}_{1}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}}\right)G+(\nicefrac{{56r}}{{\hat{\sigma}\mathsf{d}_{1}}})G^{2}+(\nicefrac{{6r}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}})G

(444)

\displaystyle=D^{2}+\left(\frac{2\mathsf{f}_{1}+6r}{\mathsf{d}_{1}}\right)G+(\nicefrac{{56r}}{{\hat{\sigma}\mathsf{d}_{1}}})G^{2},

(445)

which, together with
56r\leq\hat{\sigma}\mathsf{d}_{1}/2
— as stated in condition (
92
) —, entails

\displaystyle 2\blacksquare_{1}+x^{2}+\frac{G^{2}}{2}

\displaystyle\leq D^{2}+2\left(\frac{\mathsf{f}_{1}+3r}{\mathsf{d}_{1}}\right)G.

(446)

Proceeding similarly as before, we obtain from the displayed bound that

\displaystyle 2\blacksquare_{1}+x^{2}

\displaystyle\leq D^{2}+2\left(\frac{\mathsf{f}_{1}+3r}{\mathsf{d}_{1}}\right)^{2},

(447)

\displaystyle\frac{G^{2}}{4}

\displaystyle\leq D^{2}+4\left(\frac{\mathsf{f}_{1}+3r}{\mathsf{d}_{1}}\right)^{2}.

(448)

The proof of (
417
) follows by taking the largest of the bounds in (
436
) and (
447
). The proof of (
418
) follows by taking the largest of the bounds in (
437
) and (
448
).
∎

We will later invoke Proposition
8
in the proof of Theorem
21
when bounding the nuisance error

\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}
. Next, we prove the following lemma — an easy consequence of the first order condition when fixing

\boldsymbol{\theta}\equiv\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}
.

Lemma 33
.

For all
\mathbf{B}\in\mathds{R}^{p}
,

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\leq\langle\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}},\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})\rangle+(\hat{\sigma}\lambda)\big(\mathcal{R}(\mathbf{B})-\mathcal{R}(\hat{\mathbf{B}})\big)+\left(\|\mathcal{E}_{\mathbf{B}}^{(n)}\|_{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{2}\right)\lambda\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}).

(449)

and also

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{(n)},\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\langle\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}},\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})\rangle+(\hat{\sigma}\lambda)(\mathcal{R}(\mathbf{B})-\mathcal{R}(\hat{\mathbf{B}}))

(450)

\displaystyle+\left(\|\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})\|_{2}+\|\mathcal{E}_{\mathbf{B}}^{(n)}\|_{2}\right)\lambda\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}).

(451)

Proof.

Observe that

\displaystyle\hat{\mathbf{B}}\in

\displaystyle\argmin_{\mathbf{B}}\left\{\|\boldsymbol{y}^{(n)}-\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\mathbf{B})-\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}\|_{2}+\lambda\mathcal{R}(\mathbf{B})\right\}.

(452)

Let us define
\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{\mathbf{B}}:=\boldsymbol{y}-\mathfrak{X}(\mathbf{B})-\sqrt{n}\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}
. We first claim that

\displaystyle 0\leq\langle\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{\mathbf{B}}^{(n)},\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})\rangle+\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{\mathbf{B}}^{(n)}\|_{2}\lambda\big(\mathcal{R}(\mathbf{B})-\mathcal{R}(\hat{\mathbf{B}})\big).

(453)

It is enough to assume
\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{\mathbf{B}}\neq\mathbf{0}
. Comparing the minimality of
\hat{\mathbf{B}}
with
\mathbf{B}
and by convexity of

\boldsymbol{u}\mapsto\|\boldsymbol{u}\|_{2}
,

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\leq\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}}\|_{2}-\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}\|_{2}+\lambda\big(\mathcal{R}(\mathbf{B})-\mathcal{R}(\hat{\mathbf{B}})\big)

(454)

\displaystyle\leq-\left\langle\frac{\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}}}{\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}}\|_{2}},\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}-\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}}\right\rangle+\lambda\big(\mathcal{R}(\mathbf{B})-\mathcal{R}(\hat{\mathbf{B}})\big),

(455)

implying (
453
) since

\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}}=\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}+\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})
. Note also, that
\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{\mathbf{B}}^{(n)}=\boldsymbol{\xi}_{\mathbf{B}}^{(n)}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}
.

We now write

\displaystyle\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}}\|_{2}\lambda\big(\mathcal{R}(\mathbf{B})-\mathcal{R}(\hat{\mathbf{B}})\big)

\displaystyle=\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}\|_{2}\lambda\big(\mathcal{R}(\mathbf{B})-\mathcal{R}(\hat{\mathbf{B}})\big)

(456)

\displaystyle+(\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}}\|_{2}-\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}\|_{2})\lambda\big(\mathcal{R}(\mathbf{B})-\mathcal{R}(\hat{\mathbf{B}})\big).

(457)

By convexity of
\mathbf{W}\mapsto\mathcal{R}(\mathbf{W})
, there exist
\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{B}}\in\mathcal{R}(\mathbf{B})
such that

\displaystyle(\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}}\|_{2}-\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}\|_{2})\lambda\big(\mathcal{R}(\mathbf{B})-\mathcal{R}(\hat{\mathbf{B}})\big)

\displaystyle\leq(\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}}\|_{2}-\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}\|_{2})\lambda\left(-\langle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\langle\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{B}},\hat{\mathbf{B}}-\mathbf{B}\rangle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\rangle\right)

(458)

\displaystyle\leq(\|\mathcal{E}_{\mathbf{B}}^{(n)}\|_{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{2})\lambda\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}),

(459)

where we used that

\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}_{\mathbf{B}}^{(n)}=\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}-\mathcal{E}_{\mathbf{B}}^{(n)}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}

and

|\langle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\langle\mathbf{V}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}\rangle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\rangle|\leq\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})
. Equation (
449
) follow from the two previous displays and (
453
).

We now prove (
451
). The KKT conditions imply that there exists
\mathbf{V}\in\mathds{R}^{p}
with

\mathcal{R}^{*}(\mathbf{V})\leq 1
and
\langle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\langle\mathbf{V},\hat{\mathbf{B}}\rangle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\rangle=\mathcal{R}(\hat{\mathbf{B}})
such that, for all
\mathbf{B}\in\mathds{R}^{p}
,

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\in[n]}\left[\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{B}})+\widehat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}_{i}-y_{i}^{(n)}\right]\langle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\langle\mathbf{X}^{(n)}_{i},\mathbf{B}-\hat{\mathbf{B}}\rangle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\rangle+\lambda\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}\|_{2}\langle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\langle\mathbf{V},\mathbf{B}-\hat{\mathbf{B}}\rangle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\rangle.

(460)

Using that
\boldsymbol{y}^{(n)}=\boldsymbol{f}^{(n)}+\boldsymbol{\theta}^{*}+\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}
, we arrive at

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\leq\left\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{(n)},-\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})\right\rangle+\langle\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}},\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})\rangle-\lambda\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}\|_{2}\langle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\mathbf{V}\rangle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\rangle.

(461)

We now write

\displaystyle-\lambda\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}\|_{2}\langle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\langle\mathbf{V},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}\rangle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\rangle

\displaystyle=-\lambda\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}\|_{2}\langle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\langle\mathbf{V},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}\rangle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\rangle

(462)

\displaystyle+\lambda(\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}\|_{2}-\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}\|_{2})\langle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\langle\mathbf{V},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}\rangle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\rangle.

(463)

Using that
-\langle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\mathbf{V}\rangle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\rangle\leq\mathcal{R}(\mathbf{B})-\mathcal{R}(\hat{\mathbf{B}})
, we get that

\displaystyle-\lambda\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}\|_{2}\langle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\langle\mathbf{V},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}\rangle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\rangle\leq(\hat{\sigma}\lambda)\big(\mathcal{R}(\mathbf{B})-\mathcal{R}(\hat{\mathbf{B}})\big).

(464)

Finally, using the facts that
\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}=\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}-\mathcal{E}_{\mathbf{B}}^{(n)}-\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})
and

|\langle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\langle\mathbf{V},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}\rangle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\rangle|\leq\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})

we get

\displaystyle\lambda(\|\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}\|_{2}-\|\hat{\boldsymbol{\xi}}^{(n)}\|_{2})\langle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\langle\mathbf{V},\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}\rangle\mathchoice{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-4.5mu}{{}\mkern-2.5mu}{}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\left(\|\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})\|_{2}+\|\mathcal{E}_{\mathbf{B}}^{(n)}\|_{2}\right)\lambda\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}).

(465)

Equation (
451
) follows from the four previous displays.
∎

Using the previous lemma and
\IP
— in addition to
\MP
and
\ATP
—, we obtain the following lemma.

Lemma 34
.

Suppose conditions (i)-(iii) of Theorem
15
hold. Define the quantities

\displaystyle\hat{\blacktriangle}_{1}

\displaystyle:=\left[\mathsf{f}_{2}+\frac{\mathsf{f}_{1}\mathsf{d}_{2}}{\mathsf{d}_{1}}+\left(\mathsf{b}_{2}+\lambda+\frac{\mathsf{b}_{1}\mathsf{d}_{2}}{\mathsf{d}_{1}}\right)\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{2}+\frac{\mathsf{b}_{4}\mathsf{d}_{2}}{\mathsf{d}_{1}}\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{\sharp}+\lambda\|\mathcal{E}_{\mathbf{B}}^{(n)}\|_{2}\right]\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}),

(466)

\displaystyle\hat{\blacktriangledown}_{1}

\displaystyle:=(\hat{\sigma}\lambda)\big(\mathcal{R}(\mathbf{B})-\mathcal{R}(\hat{\mathbf{B}})\big).

(467)

Then

\displaystyle 0\leq\left[(\nicefrac{{\mathsf{f}_{1}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}})+(\nicefrac{{\mathsf{b}_{1}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}})\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{2}+(\nicefrac{{\mathsf{b}_{4}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}})\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{\sharp}+\|\mathcal{E}_{\mathbf{B}}^{(n)}\|_{2}\right]\|\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})\|_{2}+\hat{\blacktriangle}_{1}+\hat{\blacktriangledown}_{1},

(468)

and also

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{(n)}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\mathbf{B})-\boldsymbol{f}^{(n)}\|_{2}^{2}

(469)

\displaystyle+\left[(\nicefrac{{2\mathsf{f}_{1}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}})+(\nicefrac{{2\mathsf{b}_{1}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}})\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{2}+(\nicefrac{{2\mathsf{b}_{4}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}})\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{\sharp}\right]\|\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})\|_{2}

(470)

\displaystyle+2(\hat{\blacktriangle}_{1}+\hat{\blacktriangledown}_{1})+2\|\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})\|_{2}\lambda\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}).

(471)

Proof.

By the parallelogram law,

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{(n)},\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})\rangle=\frac{1}{2}\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{(n)}\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\|\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})\|_{2}^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\|\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\mathbf{B})-\boldsymbol{f}^{(n)}\|_{2}^{2}.

(472)

The previous display, (
451
) and

\|\mathfrak{M}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}},\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}})\|_{2}\leq\|\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})\|_{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{2}
imply

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{(n)}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\mathbf{B})-\boldsymbol{f}^{(n)}\|_{2}^{2}

(473)

\displaystyle+2\langle\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}},\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})\rangle

(474)

\displaystyle+2(\hat{\sigma}\lambda)(\mathcal{R}(\mathbf{B})-\mathcal{R}(\hat{\mathbf{B}}))

(475)

\displaystyle+2\|\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})\|_{2}\lambda\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})+2(\|\mathcal{E}_{\mathbf{B}}^{(n)}\|_{2}+\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{2})\lambda\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}).

(476)

By
\IP
(with variable
\mathbf{W}=\mathbb{0}
),

\displaystyle\langle-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}},\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\mathsf{b}_{1}\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}\|_{\Pi}\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{2}+\mathsf{b}_{2}\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{2}+\mathsf{b}_{4}\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}\|_{\Pi}\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{\sharp}.

(477)

By
\MP
(with variables
\mathbf{W}=\mathbb{0}
and
\boldsymbol{u}=\mathbb{0}
),

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)},\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\mathsf{f}_{1}\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}\|_{\Pi}+\mathsf{f}_{2}\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}).

(478)

By
\ATP
(with variables
\mathbf{W}=\mathbb{0}
and
\boldsymbol{u}=\mathbb{0}
),

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})\|_{2}

\displaystyle\geq\mathsf{d}_{1}\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}\|_{\Pi}-\mathsf{d}_{2}\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}).

(479)

The three previous displays imply

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}},\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}}\|_{\Pi}\left(\mathsf{b}_{1}\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{2}+\mathsf{b}_{4}\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{\sharp}+\mathsf{f}_{1}\right)+\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})(\mathsf{b}_{2}\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{2}+\mathsf{f}_{2})

(480)

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})\|_{2}\left[(\nicefrac{{\mathsf{b}_{1}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}})\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{2}+(\nicefrac{{\mathsf{b}_{4}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}})\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{\sharp}+(\nicefrac{{\mathsf{f}_{1}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}})\right]

(481)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})\left[(\nicefrac{{\mathsf{d}_{2}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}})\left(\mathsf{b}_{1}\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{2}+\mathsf{b}_{4}\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{\sharp}+\mathsf{f}_{1}\right)+\mathsf{b}_{2}\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{2}+\mathsf{f}_{2}\right].

(482)

The proof of (
471
) follows from the previous display and (
476
).

The proof of (
468
) follows from the previous display, inequality (
449
) and the fact that

\displaystyle\langle\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}},\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}},\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})\rangle-\langle\mathcal{E}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}},\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})\rangle

(483)

\displaystyle\leq\langle\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(n)}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}},\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})\rangle+\|\mathcal{E}^{(n)}_{\mathbf{B}}\|_{2}\|\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})\|_{2}.

(484)

∎

We conclude with the proof of Theorem
21
. It uses Proposition
8
and Lemma
34
.

Proof of Theorem
21
.

Let

\hat{\blacksquare}_{1}:=(\nicefrac{{\hat{\sigma}\lambda}}{{4}})\mathcal{R}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})
.
By Lemma
34
and
\|\mathcal{E}_{\mathbf{B}}^{(n)}\|_{2}\leq\hat{\sigma}\mathsf{c}_{n}
, we have

\displaystyle 0\leq\left[(\nicefrac{{\mathsf{f}_{1}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}})+(\nicefrac{{\mathsf{b}_{1}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}})\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{2}+(\nicefrac{{\mathsf{b}_{4}}}{{\mathsf{d}_{1}}})\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{\hat{\boldsymbol{\theta}}}\|_{\sharp}+\hat{\sigma}\mathsf{c}_{n}\right]\|\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})\|_{2}+\hat{\blacktriangle}_{1}+\hat{\blacktriangledown}_{1},

(485)

and also

\displaystyle 2\hat{\blacksquare}_{1}+\|\boldsymbol{\Delta}^{(n)}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\boldsymbol{\Delta}_{\mathbf{B}})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathfrak{X}^{(n)}(\mathbf{B})-\boldsymbol{f}^{(n)}\|_{2}^{2}

(486)
